Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a transfer algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 20. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 118/5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to transfer structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 20/118 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 946 + O(1/n^912). Summing over all modes and applying the transfer structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
